      Variable Definitions: 
          1. Fractional MATLAB serial day (based on 1 = January 1, 0000)
          2. Site code
          3. Measurement of RECORD
          4. Instantaneous measurement of Nitrate_mg
          5. Instantaneous measurement of TempC
          6. Instantaneous measurement of Conductivity
          7. Instantaneous measurement of SpConductivity
          8. Instantaneous measurement of pH
          9. Instantaneous measurement of ODOPerCent
         10. Instantaneous measurement of ODOMGL
         11. Instantaneous measurement of TurbidityFNU
         12. Instantaneous measurement of FDOMRFU
         13. Instantaneous measurement of FDOMQSU
         14. Aquatic CO2 meausred K30 CO2 meter
         15. Atmospheric CO2 measured by K30 CO2 meter
         16. Instantaneous stage height measured by HOBO pressure logger, corrected for barometric 
            pressure.
         17. Discharge estimated from stage height using site-specific stage-Q rating curve. 
         18. Nitrate concentration corrected for site-specific offset with laboratory analyzed grab 
            samples.
         19. Instantaneous measurement of Absorbance at 254nm from SUNA spectral data.
         20. Photosynthetically Active Radiation measured by Odyssey PAR sensor.
         21. fDOM corrected for temperature, turbidity, and absorbance.
         22. Instantaneous measurement of nitrate concentration by YSI NitraLED sensor programmed 
            with site-specific correction
